= {};\\\",\\n            sql_text(&campaign.id)\\n        ))?;\\n        if !exists.trim().is_empty() {\\n            return Ok(false);\\n        }\\n        self.sql(&format!(\\n            \\\"INSERT INTO campaigns(id,name,dm) VALUES ({},{},{});\\\",\\n            sql_text(&campaign.id),\\n            sql_text(&campaign.name),\\n            sql_text(&campaign.dm)\\n        }\\n        Ok(PlayDelegationResult::Found(entries))\\n    }\\n\\n    fn play_character_owner(\\n        &self,\\n        campaign_id: &str,\\n        actor: &str,\\n        character_id: &str,\\n    ) -> Result<PlayCharacterOwnership, ()> {\\n        let campaign = self.sql(&format!(\\n            \\\"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaigns WHERE id = {};\\\",\\n            sql_text(campaign_id)\\n        ))?;\\n        if campaign.trim().is_empty() {\\n            return Ok(PlayCharacterOwnership::UnknownCampaign);\\n        }\\n        let member = self.sql(&format!(\\n            \\\"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaign_members WHERE campaign_id = {} AND username = {};\\\",\\n            sql_text(campaign_id),\\n            sql_text(actor)\\n        ))?;\\n        if member.trim().is_empty() {\\n            return Ok(PlayCharacterOwnership::Forbidden);\\n        }\\n        let owner = self.sql(&format!(\\n            \\\"SELECT hex(owner) FROM play_campaign_character_owners WHERE campaign_id = {} AND character_id = {};\\\",\\n            sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(character_id)\\n        ))?;\\n        let owner = owner.trim();\\n        if owner.is_empty() {\\n            return Ok(PlayCharacterOwnership::Missing);\\n        }\\n        Ok(PlayCharacterOwnership::Found {\\n            owner: decode_sql_text(owner)?,\\n        })\\n    }\\n\\n    fn play_currency(&self, campaign_id: &str, actor: &str, character_id: &str) -> Result<PlayCurrency, ()> {\\n        match self.play_character_owner(campaign_id, actor, character_id)? {\\n            PlayCharacterOwnership::Found { .. } => {}\\n            PlayCharacterOwnership::UnknownCampaign => return Ok(PlayCurrency::UnknownCampaign),\\n            PlayCharacterOwnership::Forbidden => return Ok(PlayCurrency::Forbidden),\\n            PlayCharacterOwnership::Missing => return Ok(PlayCurrency::Missing),\\n            _ => return Err(()),\\n        }\\n        let gold = self.sql(&format!(\\\"SELECT gold FROM play_campaign_character_currency WHERE campaign_id = {} AND character_id = {};\\\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(character_id)))?;\\n        Ok(PlayCurrency::Found { gold: gold.trim().parse().map_err(|_| ())? })\\n    }\\n\\n    fn transfer_play_currency(&self, campaign_id: &str, actor: &str, from_character_id: &str, to_character_id: &str, gold: i64) -> Result<PlayCurrency, ()> {\\n        if from_character_id == to_character_id || gold <= 0 { return Ok(PlayCurrency::Invalid); }\\n        match self.play_character_owner(campaign_id, actor, from_character_id)? {\\n            PlayCharacterOwnership::Found { owner } if owner == actor => {}\\n            PlayCharacterOwnership::Found { .. } | PlayCharacterOwnership::Forbidden => return Ok(PlayCurrency::Forbidden),\\n            PlayCharacterOwnership::UnknownCampaign => return Ok(PlayCurrency::UnknownCampaign),\\n            PlayCharacterOwnership::Missing => return Ok(PlayCurrency::Missing),\\n            _ => return Err(()),\\n        }\\n        let destination = self.sql(&format!(\\\"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaign_members WHERE campaign_id = {} AND character_id = {};\\\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(to_character_id)))?;\\n        if destination.trim().is_empty() { return Ok(PlayCurrency::Invalid); }\\n        let from_gold: i64 = self.sql(&format!(\\\"SELECT gold FROM play_campaign_character_currency WHERE campaign_id = {} AND character_id = {};\\\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(from_character_id)))?.trim().parse().map_err(|_| ())?;\\n        if from_gold < gold { return Ok(PlayCurrency::Insufficient); }\\n        let to_gold: i64 = self.sql(&format!(\\\"SELECT gold FROM play_campaign_character_currency WHERE campaign_id = {} AND character_id = {};\\\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(to_character_id)))?.trim().parse().map_err(|_| ())?;\\n        let transfer_id: i64 = self.sql(&format!(\\\"SELECT COALESCE(MAX(id),0)+1 FROM play_campaign_currency_transfers WHERE campaign_id = {};\\\", sql_text(campaign_id)))?.trim().parse().map_err(|_| ())?;\\n        self.sql(&format!(\\\"BEGIN; UPDATE play_campaign_character_currency SET gold = gold - {} WHERE campaign_id = {} AND character_id = {}; UPDATE play_campaign_character_currency SET gold = gold + {} WHERE campaign_id = {} AND character_id = {}; INSERT INTO play_campaign_currency_transfers(campaign_id,id) VALUES ({},{}); COMMIT;\\\", gold, sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(from_character_id), gold, sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(to_character_id), sql_text(campaign_id), transfer_id))?;\\n        Ok(PlayCurrency::Transferred { from_gold: from_gold - gold, to_gold: to_gold + gold, transfer_id })\\n    }\\n\\n    fn claim_play_character(\\n        &self,\\n        campaign_id: &str,\\n        actor: &str,\\n        character_id: &str,\\n    ) -> Result<PlayCharacterOwnership, ()> {\\n = {};\\\",\\n            sql_text(&campaign.id)\\n        ))?;\\n        if !exists.trim().is_empty() {\\n            return Ok(false);\\n        }\\n        self.sql(&format!(\\n            \\\"INSERT INTO campaigns(id,name,dm) VALUES ({},{},{});\\\",\\n            sql_text(&campaign.id),\\n            sql_text(&campaign.name),\\n            sql_text(&campaign.dm)\\n        }\\n        Ok(PlayDelegationResult::Found(entries))\\n    }\\n\\n    fn play_character_owner(\\n        &self,\\n        campaign_id: &str,\\n        actor: &str,\\n        character_id: &str,\\n    ) -> Result<PlayCharacterOwnership, ()> {\\n        let campaign = self.sql(&format!(\\n            \\\"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaigns WHERE id = {};\\\",\\n            sql_text(campaign_id)\\n        ))?;\\n        if campaign.trim().is_empty() {\\n            return Ok(PlayCharacterOwnership::UnknownCampaign);\\n        }\\n        let member = self.sql(&format!(\\n            \\\"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaign_members WHERE campaign_id = {} AND username = {};\\\",\\n            sql_text(campaign_id),\\n            sql_text(actor)\\n        ))?;\\n        if member.trim().is_empty() {\\n            return Ok(PlayCharacterOwnership::Forbidden);\\n        }\\n        let owner = self.sql(&format!(\\n            \\\"SELECT hex(owner) FROM play_campaign_character_owners WHERE campaign_id = {} AND character_id = {};\\\",\\n            sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(character_id)\\n        ))?;\\n        let owner = owner.trim();\\n        if owner.is_empty() {\\n            return Ok(PlayCharacterOwnership::Missing);\\n        }\\n        Ok(PlayCharacterOwnership::Found {\\n            owner: decode_sql_text(owner)?,\\n        })\\n    }\\n\\n    fn play_currency(&self, campaign_id: &str, actor: &str, character_id: &str) -> Result<PlayCurrency, ()> {\\n        match self.play_character_owner(campaign_id, actor, character_id)? {\\n            PlayCharacterOwnership::Found { .. } => {}\\n            PlayCharacterOwnership::UnknownCampaign => return Ok(PlayCurrency::UnknownCampaign),\\n            PlayCharacterOwnership::Forbidden => return Ok(PlayCurrency::Forbidden),\\n            PlayCharacterOwnership::Missing => return Ok(PlayCurrency::Missing),\\n            _ => return Err(()),\\n        }\\n        let gold = self.sql(&format!(\\\"SELECT gold FROM play_campaign_character_currency WHERE campaign_id = {} AND character_id = {};\\\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(character_id)))?;\\n        Ok(PlayCurrency::Found { gold: gold.trim().parse().map_err(|_| ())? })\\n    }\\n\\n    fn transfer_play_currency(&self, campaign_id: &str, actor: &str, from_character_id: &str, to_character_id: &str, gold: i64) -> Result<PlayCurrency, ()> {\\n        if from_character_id == to_character_id || gold <= 0 { return Ok(PlayCurrency::Invalid); }\\n        match self.play_character_owner(campaign_id, actor, from_character_id)? {\\n            PlayCharacterOwnership::Found { owner } if owner == actor => {}\\n            PlayCharacterOwnership::Found { .. } | PlayCharacterOwnership::Forbidden => return Ok(PlayCurrency::Forbidden),\\n            PlayCharacterOwnership::UnknownCampaign => return Ok(PlayCurrency::UnknownCampaign),\\n            PlayCharacterOwnership::Missing => return Ok(PlayCurrency::Missing),\\n            _ => return Err(()),\\n        }\\n        let destination = self.sql(&format!(\\\"SELECT 1 FROM play_campaign_members WHERE campaign_id = {} AND character_id = {};\\\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(to_character_id)))?;\\n        if destination.trim().is_empty() { return Ok(PlayCurrency::Invalid); }\\n        let from_gold: i64 = self.sql(&format!(\\\"SELECT gold FROM play_campaign_character_currency WHERE campaign_id = {} AND character_id = {};\\\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(from_character_id)))?.trim().parse().map_err(|_| ())?;\\n        if from_gold < gold { return Ok(PlayCurrency::Insufficient); }\\n        let to_gold: i64 = self.sql(&format!(\\\"SELECT gold FROM play_campaign_character_currency WHERE campaign_id = {} AND character_id = {};\\\", sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(to_character_id)))?.trim().parse().map_err(|_| ())?;\\n        let transfer_id: i64 = self.sql(&format!(\\\"SELECT COALESCE(MAX(id),0)+1 FROM play_campaign_currency_transfers WHERE campaign_id = {};\\\", sql_text(campaign_id)))?.trim().parse().map_err(|_| ())?;\\n        self.sql(&format!(\\\"BEGIN; UPDATE play_campaign_character_currency SET gold = gold - {} WHERE campaign_id = {} AND character_id = {}; UPDATE play_campaign_character_currency SET gold = gold + {} WHERE campaign_id = {} AND character_id = {}; INSERT INTO play_campaign_currency_transfers(campaign_id,id) VALUES ({},{}); COMMIT;\\\", gold, sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(from_character_id), gold, sql_text(campaign_id), sql_text(to_character_id), sql_text(campaign_id), transfer_id))?;\\n        Ok(PlayCurrency::Transferred { from_gold: from_gold - gold, to_gold: to_gold + gold, transfer_id })\\n    }\\n\\n    fn claim_play_character(\\n        &self,\\n        campaign_id: &str,\\n        actor: &str,\\n        character_id: &str,\\n    ) -> Result<PlayCharacterOwnership, ()> {\\n
